INDIAN POSTS & TELEGRAPHS DEPARTMENT 
OFFICE OF THE DIRECTOR GENERAL OF POSTS & 
TELEGRAPHS 

New Delhi-110001, the 8th October 1976 

No. 25/102 /76-LT. — Postal Life Insurance Policy No. 
A. 5698, dated 8-10-74 for Rs. 10,000/- held by Shri P. G. 
Joshi having been lost from the Departmental custody, Notice 
is hereby given that the payment thereof has been stopped. 
The Dy, Director, Postal Life Insurance, Calcutta has been 
authorised to issue a. duplicate policy in favour of the 
insurant. The Public are hereby cautioned against dealing 
with the original policy. 

R. N. DEY, 
Director (PLI) 


OFFICE OF THE SALAR JUNG MUSEUM BOARD 
Hyderabad, the 8th June 1976 

No. U-8/75/114. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1) of section 28 of the Salar Jung Museum 
Act, 1961 (26 of 1961), the Salar Juug Museum Board, 
Hyderabad with the previous approval of the Central Gov- 
ernment, hereby make the following regulations further to 
amend Ihe Salar Jung Museum Regulations 1962 namely : — 

1. (1) These Regulations may be called the Salar Jung 

Museum (Amendment) Regulations, 1976; 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the official Gazette, 

2. Add the following to the existing Regulation No. 22 of 
the Salar Jung Museum Board Regulations 1962. 

“(iii) A representative of the Ministry of Finance.”

Secretary Chairman
